# Chapter 7.1  [SPINE]
## AI industry mein paisa kaun kamata hai

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Book 1 Chapter 6.9 ka niyam bolo.

**Kabir:** Paisa neeche se aata hai, jab ek insaan apni zaroorat ke liye deta hai. Aur woh wahan rukta hai jahan sabse kam vikalp hain.

**Madhav:** Ab AI pe lagao. Neeche se upar chalo. Sabse neeche kya hai?

**Kabir:** Chip. Woh machinein jinpe yeh sab chalta hai.

**Madhav:** Uske vikalp kitne hain?

**Kabir:** Bahut kam. Aur unhe banane wale bhi bahut kam.

**Madhav:** Toh wahan paisa rukega ya nikal jaayega?

**Kabir:** Rukega.

**Madhav:** Uske upar?

**Kabir:** Woh jagah jahan machinein rakhi hain. Cloud.

**Madhav:** Uske vikalp?

**Kabir:** Kuch badi companies. Toh wahan bhi paisa rukega, lekin kam.

**Madhav:** Uske upar?

**Kabir:** Model banane wale.

**Madhav:** Unke vikalp?

**Kabir:** Kuch companies hain jo bade model bana sakti hain. Toh scarcity hai.

**Madhav:** Achha. Ab ek problem. Kuch models muft mein bant rahe hain. Chapter 2.9. Toh?

**Kabir:** Toh unka daam gir raha hai.

**Madhav:** Aur agar do models lagbhag ek jaise kaam karein, ek mehnga ek sasta?

**Kabir:** Log sasta lenge.

**Madhav:** Toh us parat pe kya ho raha hai?

**Kabir:** Vikalp badh rahe hain. Toh scarcity gir rahi hai.

**Madhav:** Aur jab scarcity girti hai toh?

**Kabir:** Paisa wahan rukna band ho jaata hai. Woh aage nikal jaata hai.

**Madhav:** Kahan?

**Kabir:** Ya toh neeche, chip wale ke paas. Ya upar.

**Madhav:** Upar kya hai?

**Kabir:** Woh products jo model pe khade hain.

**Madhav:** Unke vikalp?

**Kabir:** Bahut. Koi bhi bana sakta hai.

**Madhav:** Toh wahan paisa rukega?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Jab tak unke paas kuch aur na ho.

**Madhav:** Aur "kuch aur" kya ho sakta hai?

**Kabir:** Unka apna data. Ya unke users ka jaal. Ya woh cheezein jinse nikalna mushkil ho.

**Madhav:** Bas. Ab poori tasveer bolo.

**Kabir:** Paisa un do jagahon pe ruk raha hai jahan vikalp kam hain: sabse neeche chip, aur sabse upar woh products jinke paas apna kuch hai. Beech ki parat nichudti ja rahi hai.

### Naam

Poori parat, neeche se upar, aur har ek pe wahi sawal: **iske kitne vikalp hain?**

```
CHIP                  vikalp bahut kam       →  paisa rukta hai
                      banane wale gine-chune

BIJLI + JAGAH         seemit, aur badhana dheema   →  rukta hai
                      (yeh 2024 ke baad se ek asli seema ban gayi)

CLOUD                 kuch badi companies    →  thoda rukta hai

MODEL                 kuch companies, lekin  →  gir raha hai
                      muft models aa rahe hain

APP / PRODUCT         koi bhi bana sakta hai →  nahi rukta
                      jab tak uske paas kuch aur na ho
```

Aur woh "kuch aur," jo product wali parat pe paisa rok sakta hai:

```
APNA DATA        jo kisi aur ke paas nahi hai   (Ch 2.6, 6.6)
USERS KA JAAL    log wahan hain, isliye log wahan hain
                 (Book 1, Ch 4.1)
KAAM MEIN GHUSA  aapke roz ke kaam ka hissa ban gaya,
                 nikalna mehnga (Book 1, Ch 6.5)
BHAROSA          jahan galti ki keemat zyada hai
```

Ab teen baatein jo iss tasveer se nikalti hain.

**Ek: model wali parat sabse zyada charcha mein hai aur wahan paisa sabse tezi se gir raha hai.**

Kyunki teen cheezein ek saath ho rahi hain: models muft bant rahe hain, alag models lagbhag ek jaise kaam kar rahe hain, aur daam har saal tezi se gir raha hai.

Jo cheez badalne laayak aur sasti ho jaati hai, uspe paisa nahi rukta. **Yeh Book 1 Chapter 6.9 hai, bina kisi badlav ke.**

**Do: sabse neeche wali parat sabse mazboot hai.**

Chip banana mushkil hai, mehnga hai, aur usmein saalon lagte hain. Aur upar chahe kuch bhi ho, sabko chip chahiye.

**Teen: data ab ek scarce cheez ban rahi hai.**

Shuruaat mein woh muft tha. Ab aasan data khatam ho raha hai, log apna data band kar rahe hain, aur AI ka likha data mila hua hai (Ch 2.7).

Toh woh data jo kisi ek jagah ikattha hai, aur pakka insaan ka likha hai, woh mehnga hota ja raha hai.

**Jo cheez muft se scarce banti hai, uske maalik ki taakat achanak badh jaati hai.**

### Asli duniya se

2023 se 2026 ke beech, ek hi kaam ka daam har saal tezi se gira. Jo kaam pehle mehnga tha, woh ab uska ek chhota hissa hai.

Aur usi daur mein AI chips banane wali company duniya ki sabse badi companies mein aa gayi.

Dono baatein ek saath sach hain, aur woh ek hi tasveer hain: **beech ki parat sasti hui, aur neeche wali parat mehngi.**

Ab uska aapke liye matlab: agar aap kuch bana rahe ho jo model pe khada hai, toh aapka kharcha har saal girega. Yeh achhi khabar hai.

Aur uske saath: aapka product bhi utna hi aasan hoga banane mein, sabke liye. **Jo cheez aapke liye sasti hui, woh aapke mukable ke liye bhi sasti hui.**

Toh sawal wahi purana reh jaata hai: **aapke paas aisa kya hai jo kisi aur ke paas nahi?**

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki AI ka paisa model banane mein hai.**

Model banana bahut mehnga hai aur usmein rukta bahut kam hai, kyunki uska daam gir raha hai aur uske vikalp badh rahe hain.

Doosri galti: **yeh sochna ki "AI wrapper" ek gaali hai.**

Log kehte hain ki jo product model pe khada hai woh "sirf ek wrapper" hai.

Book 1 Chapter 2.9 se: **product ka bada hissa model ke bahar hota hai.** Kaunsa data, kaunse tools, kaunsi jaanch, kaunsa anubhav.

Sahi sawal yeh nahi hai ki woh wrapper hai ya nahi. Sahi sawal wahi purana hai: **iske kitne vikalp hain?**

Ek wrapper jiske paas apna data hai aur jo kisi ke roz ke kaam mein ghus gaya hai, woh mazboot hai. Ek wrapper jise koi ek hafte mein bana sakta hai, woh nahi.

Teesri galti: **yeh maan lena ki aaj ki tasveer kal bhi wahi rahegi.**

Do saal pehle sabse badi scarcity model mein thi. Aaj woh chip aur bijli mein hai. Do saal baad woh kahin aur ho sakti hai.

**Sawal sthir hai, jawab nahi.**

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Aap ek AI product banate ho. Ek saal mein model ka daam paanch guna sasta ho jaata hai. Aapke liye achha ya bura?

> **Jawab:** Dono, aur doosra hissa log bhool jaate hain.
>
> **Achha:** aapka kharcha gira. Jo cheezein pehle mehngi thin, ab ban sakti hain.
>
> **Bura:** aapke mukable ke liye bhi gira. Aur woh cheez jo pehle sirf aap kar sakte the kyunki mehngi thi, ab koi bhi kar sakta hai.
>
> **Jo cheez sabke liye sasti ho jaati hai, woh kisi ki bhi bacha nahi hoti.** Aapka bachaav us cheez mein hona chahiye jo sasti nahi hui.

**2. (samajh check)** Do AI products hain. Ek mein log apna data daalte hain aur woh wahan jama hota hai. Doosra har baar naya kaam karta hai. Kaunsa mazboot hai?

> **Jawab:** Pehla, aur farak waqt ke saath badhta hai.
>
> Pehle mein har din jama hone wala data ek deewar ban raha hai. Nikalne ki keemat badh rahi hai, aur jo jama hua woh kisi aur ke paas nahi hai.
>
> Doosre mein har din shuruaat se shuru hota hai. Koi jama nahi ho raha.
>
> **Yeh Book 1 Ch 0.2 ka leverage hai: kya aapka kaam jama hota hai, ya har baar khatam ho jaata hai?** Ravi aur Anil, ab AI products mein.

**3. (jodne wala)** Book 1 Ch 3.6 mein tha ki companies apni cheezein muft kyun deti hain. AI models pe woh kaise lagta hai?

> **Jawab:** Wahi teesri wajah, sabse bade paimane pe: **neeche wali parat pe kabza.**
>
> Agar aapka model muft hai aur sab uspe cheezein banate hain, toh woh sabki neev ban jaata hai. Aur jo neev banata hai, woh uske aas-paas ki har cheez ki shakal tay karta hai.
>
> Aur wahi keemat bhi hai: aapne apni sabse mehngi cheez de di, aur ab paisa kahin aur se banana padega. Cloud se, ya product se, ya us data se jo aapke paas aata hai.
>
> **Jo muft de raha hai, uska ek plan hai. Woh plan dhoondhna aapka kaam hai.**

---

# Chapter 7.2  [SPINE]
## Ek aadmi 2026 mein kya bana sakta hai

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Book 1 Chapter 8.2 mein chaar pehchaanein thin: kya banana aasan hai aur kya mushkil. Unhe bolo.

**Kabir:** Sahi jawab saaf hai ya dhundhla. Waqt ki sakhti. Paisa ya kanoon juda hai ya nahi. Aur naya kaam ya purani cheez chhoona.

**Madhav:** Ab batao ki AI ne inmein se kaunsi badli.

**Kabir:** Shayad pehli? Ab sahi jawab dhundhla ho toh bhi kaam ho sakta hai?

**Madhav:** Kya AI ka jawab bharosemand hai jab sahi jawab dhundhla ho?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Wahin toh woh sabse zyada bhatakta hai.

**Madhav:** Toh pehli pehchaan badli?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Woh waisi ki waisi hai.

**Madhav:** Doosri? Waqt ki sakhti?

**Kabir:** Nahi. AI dheema hai, tez nahi.

**Madhav:** Teesri? Paisa aur kanoon?

**Kabir:** Aur mushkil ho gayi, kyunki ab ek aur cheez hai jo galti kar sakti hai.

**Madhav:** Chauthi? Purani cheez chhoona?

**Kabir:** Woh shayad thodi aasan hui. AI purana code padh kar samjha deta hai.

**Madhav:** Thodi. Toh chaaron mein se koi bhi poori tarah nahi badli. Phir badla kya?

**Kabir:** Likhne ki speed.

**Madhav:** Aur woh kitna hissa tha?

**Kabir:** Pata nahi. Aadha?

**Madhav:** Ek naye product mein likhna shayad chauthai hai. Baaki kya hai?

**Kabir:** Tay karna ki kya banana hai. Design. Jaanch. Chalana. Logon tak pahunchana.

**Madhav:** Toh agar likhna do guna tez ho gaya, toh poora kaam kitna tez hua?

**Kabir:** Bahut kam. Shayad ek chauthai.

**Madhav:** Toh phir log kyun kehte hain ki sab kuch badal gaya?

**Kabir:** Kyunki shuruaat bahut tez ho gayi. Pehla chalta hua roop ab ek din mein ban jaata hai.

**Madhav:** Aur aage ka kaam?

**Kabir:** Woh utna hi hai.

**Madhav:** Bas. **Shuruaat sasti hui hai, poora karna nahi.**

### Naam

Chaar pehchaanein, aur AI ka unpe asar:

```
1.  SAHI JAWAB SAAF HAI YA NAHI      lagbhag nahi badla
    dhundhla jawab wahin hai jahan AI sabse kamzor hai

2.  WAQT KI SAKHTI                    nahi badla
    AI dheema hai

3.  PAISA YA KANOON                   thoda mushkil hua
    ek aur cheez jo galti kar sakti hai, aur jiski
    galti samjhayi nahi ja sakti (Ch 2.1)

4.  NAYA YA PURANA                    thoda aasan hua
    purana code padhna aur samajhna sasta ho gaya
```

Aur jo sach mein badla:

```
LIKHNA           bahut sasta          ← sabse bada badlav
PADHNA/SAMAJHNA  bahut sasta          ← kam charcha, bada asar
PEHLA ROOP       ghanton mein          ← isliye lagta hai sab badal gaya

DESIGN           utna hi
JAANCH           utni hi (aur zyada zaroori, Ch 5.4)
CHALANA          utna hi (poori Book 1)
LOGON TAK        utna hi (Book 1, Ch 8.5)
```

Ab do baatein jo iss chapter ki jaan hain.

**Ek: ek aadmi ab wahi bana sakta hai jo pehle chhoti team banati thi, agar kaam pehli pehchaan mein aasan ho.**

Yeh sach mein bada badlav hai. Woh cheezein jo pehle "iske liye do log chahiye" thin, ab ek aadmi kar leta hai.

Aur woh pehli pehchaan wahi rehti hai: **jinka sahi jawab saaf hai.**

**Do: aur wahi baat aapke mukable ke liye bhi sach hai.**

Yeh Chapter 7.1 ka wahi sabak hai. Jo aapke liye sasta hua, woh sabke liye sasta hua.

Toh "main isse jaldi bana sakta hoon" ab koi bachaav nahi hai. Woh do saal pehle ek bachaav tha.

Jo bachaav hai woh wahi hai jo pehle bhi tha, aur ab pehle se zyada:

```
kya aapke paas aisa data hai jo kisi aur ke paas nahi?
kya aap us problem ko jaante ho jise koi aur nahi jaanta?
kya log aapke paas hain? (Book 1, Ch 4.1)
kya aapka kaam jama hota hai, ya har baar khatam?
```

**Banane ki kshamta sasti hui. Jaanna ki kya banana hai, mehngi hi rahi.**

### Asli duniya se

Ek jaani-pehchani cheez baar baar dikhi hai: koi ek aadmi ek hafte mein ek product banata hai, uska demo bahut achha hota hai, aur woh product kabhi asli istemaal tak nahi pahunchta.

Aur uski wajah lagbhag hamesha wahi teen hoti hain: jaanch nahi thi, chalana mushkil nikla, ya logon tak pahunchne ka koi rasta nahi tha.

Yaani woh teen cheezein jo AI ne nahi badli.

Aur ulta bhi hua hai: log jo pehle se kisi problem ko gehrai se jaante the, unhone AI ke saath woh cheezein banayi jo pehle unke bas ke bahar thin.

**Farak yeh nahi tha ki kaun AI istemaal kar raha tha. Farak yeh tha ki kaun jaanta tha ki kya banana hai.**

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **shuruaat ki speed ko poore kaam ki speed samajhna.**

Pehla chalta hua roop ab bahut jaldi ban jaata hai, aur woh chaunkane wala hai. Aur uske baad ka kaam lagbhag utna hi hai.

Iska seedha nateeja: log aadhe-adhoore products se bhar jaate hain, aur unmein se kuch bhi poora nahi hota.

Doosri galti: **yeh sochna ki AI ne "coding seekhna" bekaar kar diya.**

Book 1 Chapter 7.1 se: aapko yeh jaanna padta hai ki jo bana woh sahi hai ya nahi. Aur AI apni galti khud nahi pehchan sakta (Ch 5.2).

Toh jaanne ki zaroorat kam nahi hui. Woh khisak gayi: **likhne se jaanchne ki taraf.**

Teesri galti: **yeh maan lena ki jo aap bana sakte ho woh koi aur nahi bana sakta.**

Do saal pehle "main isse bana sakta hoon" ek bachaav tha. Ab woh nahi hai.

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Aapko ek idea aaya aur aapne ek hafte mein uska chalta hua roop bana liya. Ab sabse bada khatra kya hai?

> **Jawab:** Yeh maan lena ki mushkil hissa ho gaya.
>
> Jo hua woh sabse aasan hissa tha, aur wahi hissa sabse zyada sasta hua hai.
>
> Jo bacha hai: kya woh sach mein kisi ki problem hal karta hai, kya woh chalta rahega, kya log use dhoondh payenge, aur kya koi aur do din mein wahi nahi bana lega.
>
> **Book 1 Ch 8.5 ka wahi sawal: jeet cheez ki quality se nahi aati.**

**2. (samajh check)** Do log ek hi din ek hi tarah ka AI product banate hain. Ek chal jaata hai, doosra nahi. Sabse sambhavit wajah?

> **Jawab:** Ek jaanta tha ki kiski problem hal kar raha hai, doosra nahi.
>
> Jab banane ka daam gir jaata hai, toh farak banane mein nahi rehta. Woh us jaankari mein reh jaata hai jo banane se pehle thi.
>
> Aur woh jaankari kahin muft nahi milti. Woh us kaam ke andar rehne se aati hai.
>
> **Isliye AI ne un logon ko sabse zyada faayda diya jo pehle se kisi problem ke andar the**, na ki un logon ko jo ek tool dhoondh rahe the.

**3. (jodne wala)** Book 1 Ch 0.2 ke teen dials pe yeh sab lagao.

> **Jawab:**
>
> **Size:** nahi badla. Problem kitni badi hai, yeh AI se tay nahi hota.
>
> **Scarcity:** yahan sabse bada badlav hua, aur woh khisak gaya. "Code likh sakta hoon" ki scarcity gir gayi. "Jaanta hoon ki kya banana hai" aur "jaanch sakta hoon ki yeh sahi hai" ki scarcity badh gayi.
>
> **Leverage:** aasan hua. Ek aadmi ab woh cheez bana sakta hai jo uske bina chalti rahe.
>
> **Toh formula wahi hai. Bas ek dial ka matlab badal gaya.** Aur jo log purane matlab pe khade hain, unke paanv ke neeche se zameen khisak rahi hai.

---

# Chapter 7.3  [SPINE]
## Leverage ab kahan baitha hai

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Book 1 Chapter 0.2 mein Anil ne naali khodi thi. Uska leverage kya tha?

**Kabir:** Usne aisa kaam kiya jo uske bina chalta raha.

**Madhav:** Aaj ke zamaane mein woh naali kya hai?

**Kabir:** Software. Ya koi system.

**Madhav:** Aur agar software likhna lagbhag muft ho jaaye?

**Kabir:** Toh naali khodna sasta ho gaya.

**Madhav:** Aur agar naali khodna sabke liye sasta hai, toh naali ka kya mol bacha?

**Kabir:** Kam. Kyunki ab sab khod sakte hain.

**Madhav:** Toh leverage khatam ho gaya?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Woh kahin aur chala gaya hoga.

**Madhav:** Kahan? Sochte hain. Anil ki naali ka mol kis baat mein tha?

**Kabir:** Ki woh chalti rahi.

**Madhav:** Aur kis baat mein?

**Kabir:** Ki gaon ko paani chahiye tha.

**Madhav:** Aur teesri?

**Kabir:** Ki woh khod sakta tha aur baaki nahi.

**Madhav:** Ab teeno pe AI lagao. Kaunsi kamzor hui?

**Kabir:** Teesri. Ab bahut log khod sakte hain.

**Madhav:** Pehli?

**Kabir:** Wahi hai. Cheez ab bhi chalti rehti hai.

**Madhav:** Doosri?

**Kabir:** Woh bhi wahi hai. Gaon ko paani ab bhi chahiye.

**Madhav:** Toh leverage teen mein se do jagah abhi bhi hai.

**Kabir:** Haan. Sirf "main kar sakta hoon" wala hissa sasta hua.

**Madhav:** Aur ab ek nayi baat. Anil ki naali khud badi nahi hoti thi. Kya aisi cheezein hain jo khud badhti hain?

**Kabir:** Network. Book 1 Chapter 4.1. Har naya aane wala use mazboot karta hai.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Data. Jitna zyada log istemaal karein, utna zyada data jama ho.

**Madhav:** Aur woh data kis kaam aata hai?

**Kabir:** Cheez ko behtar karne. Jo aur log laata hai. Jo aur data laata hai.

**Madhav:** Toh ab do kism ke leverage ho gaye.

**Kabir:** Ek jo bana kar rakh diya jaata hai. Aur ek jo apne aap badhta hai.

**Madhav:** Aur kaunsa mazboot hai jab banana sasta ho gaya ho?

**Kabir:** Doosra. Kyunki pehla koi bhi dobara bana lega.

### Naam

Leverage ke teen darje, sabse kamzor se sabse mazboot:

```
1.  BANAYI HUI CHEEZ (sthir)
    ek baar banao, chalti rehti hai
    ── AI ne ise sasta kar diya, isliye kamzor hua
       kyunki koi bhi dobara bana sakta hai

2.  JAMA HONE WALI CHEEZ
    har din kuch jud raha hai jo kal nahi tha
    data, itihaas, bharosa, aadat
    ── AI ne ise nahi chhua

3.  KHUD BADHNE WALI CHEEZ
    har naya user use sabke liye behtar bana deta hai
    network, aur woh systems jo istemaal se seekhte hain
    ── sabse mazboot, aur sabse mushkil shuru karna
```

Aur ab woh line jo iss chapter ka nichod hai:

> **AI ne pehle darje ka leverage sasta kar diya. Doosre aur teesre ko chhua bhi nahi.**

Isliye jo log pehle darje pe khade the, unki zameen khisak rahi hai. Aur jo doosre ya teesre pe hain, unke liye AI sirf ek sasta auzaar hai.

Ab ek aur farak, jo Book 1 Chapter 6.4 se aata hai aur ab sabse zyada mayne rakhta hai:

```
apni naali            koi aapko hata nahi sakta
kisi aur ki naali se  woh kal band kar sakta hai
paani lena
```

Aaj lagbhag har AI product kisi aur ke model pe khada hai. Woh kisi aur ki naali hai.

Iska matlab yeh nahi ki aisa nahi karna chahiye. Lagbhag sab yehi kar rahe hain, aur usme samajhdaari hai.

Matlab yeh hai ki **aapka leverage us naali mein nahi ho sakta.** Woh us cheez mein hona chahiye jo aapki hai: aapka data, aapke log, aapki jama hui cheez.

### Asli duniya se

2023 se lagbhag har hafte koi AI product aata hai jo kisi bade model ke upar ek achha interface rakhta hai.

Unmein se zyadatar chale nahi. Aur unki wajah lagbhag hamesha ek hi hai: unke paas sirf pehle darje ka leverage tha, jo ab sasta ho chuka hai.

Aur unmein se jo chale, unmein aksar ek hi cheez saanjha thi: unke paas kuch aisa tha jo model se nahi aata tha. Ek khaas data, ek khaas group ke log, ya kisi kaam ki gehri samajh jo bahar se nahi milti.

**Yeh Book 1 Chapter 8.5 hai, bina kisi badlav ke: jeet cheez ki quality se nahi aati.** Aur ab jab cheez banana lagbhag sabke liye mumkin hai, toh woh baat pehle se zyada sach hai.

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki AI aane se leverage badh gaya.**

Aapka leverage badha, aur sabka badha. Jab sabka badhta hai toh kisi ka nahi badhta, us rishte mein jo mayne rakhta hai.

Doosri galti: **pehle darje ke leverage ko abhi bhi bachaav samajhna.**

"Maine yeh bana liya hai" ab ek chhota bachaav hai. Woh do saal pehle bada tha.

Teesri galti, aur yeh sabse mehngi hai: **kisi aur ki naali pe apna poora ghar bana lena.**

Agar aapka poora product ek company ke model, unke daam, aur unke niyam pe khada hai, toh aapka leverage unka hai, aapka nahi. Book 1 Chapter 6.4 ka wahi sabak, sabse bade paimane pe.

Aur uska ilaaj bhi wahi hai: **aisi cheez rakho jo unki nahi hai.**

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Do products hain. Ek har baar naya jawab banata hai. Doosre mein har istemaal se kuch jama hota hai. Ek saal baad kya farak hoga?

> **Jawab:** Pehla wahin khada hoga jahan shuru mein tha. Doosra ek saal aage hoga, aur woh doori har din badhegi.
>
> Aur pehle ko koi bhi dobara bana sakta hai. Doosre ko dobara banane ke liye ek saal ka istemaal chahiye.
>
> **Isliye sabse zaroori design wala sawal yeh hai: har istemaal se kya jama ho raha hai?** Agar jawab "kuch nahi" hai, toh aap har roz shuruaat se shuru kar rahe ho.

**2. (samajh check)** Aapka product ek badi company ke model pe khada hai. Kya yeh galti hai?

> **Jawab:** Nahi, aur woh lagbhag har product karta hai.
>
> Galti tab hoti hai jab aapka **poora** mol usi pe khada ho.
>
> Do sawal poochne laayak hain: agar woh kal daam das guna kar de toh? Agar woh kal khud wahi product bana le toh?
>
> Agar dono ka jawab "sab khatam" hai, toh aapke paas apna kuch hai hi nahi.
>
> **Kisi aur ki naali istemaal karo. Uspe apna ghar mat banao.**

**3. (jodne wala)** Book 1 Ch 0.2 mein Ravi bees saal chala aur uska kaam roz khatam ho gaya. Aaj Ravi kaun hai?

> **Jawab:** Woh jiska kaam har baar shuruaat se shuru hota hai aur kuch jama nahi karta.
>
> Aur woh sirf mehnat wala kaam nahi hai. Woh ek aisa product bhi ho sakta hai jo har roz naye users ko wahi cheez deta hai, aur unse kuch nahi seekhta.
>
> Anil aaj woh hai jiska kaam jama hota hai, ya jise har naya user aur mazboot karta hai.
>
> **Aur AI ne matka uthana sasta kar diya hai, na ki naali khodna bekaar.** Jo log matka uthate reh gaye, woh ab pehle se tez uthate hain, aur unka ceiling wahi hai.

# Chapter 7.4  [SPINE]
## Kisi bhi nayi cheez ko 60 second mein rakhna

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Ek khabar aati hai. "Nayi technique se model ka context das guna bada ho gaya." Kya poochoge?

**Kabir:** Kaunsi parat? Yeh attention ke hisaab wali problem hai. *(3.3)*

**Madhav:** Kaunsa dard?

**Kabir:** Ki attention ka kaam varg mein badhta hai, aur memory bhi bharti hai. *(4.4)*

**Madhav:** Kaunsa sauda?

**Kabir:** Pata nahi. Lekin kuch toh hoga.

**Madhav:** Kya ho sakta hai?

**Kabir:** Shayad woh har shabd ko har shabd se nahi joddta. Toh kuch rishte chhoot jaayenge.

**Madhav:** Toh aapne bina jaane hi andaza laga liya ki keemat kahan hogi.

**Kabir:** Kyunki agar kuch muft mein bada hua hai, toh kahin kuch chhoda gaya hai.

**Madhav:** Ab doosri khabar. "Naya model kam parameters mein zyada achha hai."

**Kabir:** Parat: training. Dard: bade model mehnge hain, chalane mein aur banane mein.

**Madhav:** Sauda?

**Kabir:** Shayad zyada data, ya zyada mehnat training mein. Ya woh kisi khaas kaam mein achha hai aur baaki mein nahi.

**Madhav:** Teesri. "Naya agent framework."

**Kabir:** Parat: model ke bahar. Dard: loop banana, tools sambhalna, kadam ka record rakhna. *(6.4)*

**Madhav:** Sauda?

**Kabir:** Ek aur cheez pe nirbharta. Aur woh mere aur model ke beech mein aa jaayega.

**Madhav:** Ab chauthi, aur yeh tedhi hai. "AI ab reasoning kar sakta hai."

**Kabir:** Yeh... kaunsi parat hai?

**Madhav:** Aap hi batao. Aisa kya badla hoga?

**Kabir:** Shayad woh jawab dene se pehle apne liye kuch likh leta hai. Chapter 5.2.

**Madhav:** Toh kya andar kuch naya aaya?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Woh cheez likhawat mein aa gayi jahan woh use dekh sakta hai.

**Madhav:** Aur sauda?

**Kabir:** Woh zyada tokens likhega. Toh mehnga aur dheema hoga.

**Madhav:** Bas. Char khabarein, char baar wahi teen sawal, aur har baar aap kuch bata paaye.

### Naam

Teen sawal. Yeh Book 1 Chapter 8.1 se aaye hain aur yahan bhi wahi hain.

```
1.  YEH KAUNSI PARAT PE HAI?
       chip / machine
       training (data, loss, weights)
       architecture (attention, layers)
       inference (prefill, decode, chunav)
       model ke bahar (RAG, tools, loop, filter)
       product / interface

2.  YEH KIS DARD KE LIYE BANI THI?
       agar aap dard nahi bata sakte, toh do mein se ek hai:
       aapne samjha nahi, ya wahan dard tha hi nahi

3.  ISKA SAUDA KYA HAI?
       har cheez kuch deti hai aur kuch leti hai
       agar nahi dikh raha toh aapne dhoondha nahi
```

Aur Book 2 ke liye ek chautha sawal, jo bahut kaam ka hai:

```
4.  YEH MODEL KE ANDAR HAI YA BAHAR?

    ANDAR   nayi training, naya data, nayi architecture
            → mehnga, dheema, aur sirf woh company kar sakti hai
            → asar gehra, aur pehle se andaza mushkil

    BAHAR   naye tools, behtar RAG, behtar nirdesh, naya loop
            → sasta, tez, koi bhi kar sakta hai
            → asar turant dikhta hai
```

Chautha sawal isliye zaroori hai ki **pichhle do saal ki bahut si "AI behtar ho gaya" wali khabarein model ke bahar ki thin.**

Aur woh do bilkul alag baatein hain:

```
model behtar hua       →  sabke liye behtar hua, saath saath
use tool mil gaya      →  aap bhi kal wahi laga sakte ho
```

### Char udaharan, poore

```
"CONTEXT 10 LAKH TOKEN"
parat    inference / architecture
dard     attention varg mein badhti hai, memory bharti hai
sauda    daam aur waqt, aur beech ka hissa aksar dab jaata hai
andar    andar
matlab   suvidha hai, hal nahi. Chun kar bhejna phir bhi behtar.

"NAYA MODEL 5 GUNA SASTA"
parat    inference
dard     kharcha, jo asli karobaari seema hai
sauda    aksar chhota model, toh mushkil kaamon mein kamzor
andar    andar
matlab   yeh guna wala badlav hai. Naye kaam mumkin ho jaate hain.

"YEH AI AAPKA POORA KAAM KAR DEGA"
parat    product
dard     log poora kaam chahte hain, tukda nahi
sauda    lambe kaam mein galtiyan judti hain (6.4, 6.8)
andar    bahar
matlab   poochho ki har kadam pe jaanch kya hai. Nahi hai toh shak karo.

"MODEL AB APNI GALTI PAKAD LETA HAI"
parat    dhalai + inference
dard     woh apni galti nahi pakad sakta tha (5.2)
sauda    zyada tokens, isliye mehnga aur dheema
andar    beech mein
matlab   kaafi behtar hua hai, aur "khud jaanch" ab bhi bahar se
         hi aati hai. Ek cheez khud ko nahi jaanch sakti.
```

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **har khabar ko ek nayi kism ki cheez samajhna.**

Zyadatar khabarein in paanch mein se ek hoti hain:

```
model bada hua
data behtar hua
dhalai behtar hui
model ke bahar kuch naya juda
daam gira
```

Aur unka matlab bilkul alag hota hai. Daam girna aksar sabse bada asar rakhta hai aur sabse kam charcha paata hai.

Doosri galti: **demo ko kaabiliyat samajhna.**

Ek demo chuna hua hota hai. Woh dikhata hai ki cheez achhe case mein kya kar sakti hai.

Aapko yeh jaanna hai ki woh **aapke** case mein kya karti hai, jo aksar bura case hota hai. Yeh Chapter 5.3 hai: bees sawal ka apna test.

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Khabar: "naya model tark ke test mein sabse aage hai." Chaar sawal chalao.

> **Jawab:**
>
> **Parat:** dhalai aur inference, aksar dono.
>
> **Dard:** lambe tark mein galtiyan judti thin (6.4, 5.2).
>
> **Sauda:** woh jawab dene se pehle bahut likhta hai. Toh mehnga aur dheema. Ek chhote sawal ka jawab bhi ab teen guna waqt le sakta hai.
>
> **Andar ya bahar:** beech mein. Uski training badli hai, aur uska istemaal karne ka tareeka bhi.
>
> **Aapke liye matlab:** mushkil kaamon mein behtar. Aasan kaamon mein sirf mehnga. Aur benchmark ka number aapke kaam ke baare mein kuch nahi kehta (5.3).

**2. (samajh check)** Khabar: "yeh AI aapke poore codebase ko samajh leta hai." Kya poochoge?

> **Jawab:** Sabse pehle: **kya woh poora bheja jaata hai, ya chun kar?**
>
> Agar poora bheja jaata hai, toh woh mehnga hoga, dheema hoga, aur beech ka hissa dabega (4.4).
>
> Agar chun kar bheja jaata hai, toh yeh RAG hai (6.2), aur asli sawal yeh hai ki **chunna kitna achha hai.** Wahin uski poori quality baithi hai.
>
> "Samajh leta hai" ek product ka vaakya hai. Andar hamesha in do mein se ek hai.

**3. (jodne wala)** Book 1 Ch 8.5 mein jeet ki paanch wajahein thin, aur unmein "achha hona" nahi thi. Kya woh AI pe bhi lagti hain?

> **Jawab:** Poori tarah, aur woh abhi saamne ho raha hai.
>
> **Pehle se wahan hona:** jo AI aapke pehle se istemaal hone wale tool mein aa gaya, woh jeet raha hai, chahe alag se behtar cheez maujood ho.
>
> **Log wahan hain:** jahan aapki team, aapka code, aapka data pehle se hai.
>
> **Aas-paas ka saaman:** jiske aas-paas sabse zyada cheezein bani hain.
>
> **Aasani:** jo lagane mein sabse aasan hai.
>
> **Aur "kaunsa model benchmark mein aage hai" us list mein kahin nahi hai.** Yeh woh baat hai jo khabar padhne walon ko sabse zyada chaunkati hai.

---

# Chapter 7.5  [SPINE]
## Tech news kaise follow karein

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Kitaab ke shuru mein aapki shikayat kya thi?

**Kabir:** Ki har hafte kuch naya sunta hoon jo baaki sabko pehle se pata tha.

**Madhav:** Ab wahi problem alag shakal le legi. Ab aap sab kuch samajh sakte ho, toh aap sab kuch padhna chahoge.

**Kabir:** Aur uska koi ant nahi hai.

**Madhav:** Roz kitni AI ki khabarein aati hain?

**Kabir:** Bahut. Sau se zyada.

**Madhav:** Unmein se kitni mayne rakhti hain?

**Kabir:** Shayad hafte mein ek.

**Madhav:** Toh sawal kya hai?

**Kabir:** Kaunsi ek?

**Madhav:** Ab ek chhannan banate hain. Book 1 Chapter 2.2 se: kaunsa badlav mayne rakhta hai?

**Kabir:** Guna wala. Pratishat wala nahi.

**Madhav:** Toh pehla chhannan?

**Kabir:** Agar kuch das guna sasta, tez, ya bada hua hai toh dekho. 20 percent behtar ko chhod do.

**Madhav:** Achha. Doosra. Ek naya model aaya jo kisi benchmark mein aage hai. Mayne rakhta hai?

**Kabir:** Nahi, aksar. Woh har mahine hota hai. Aur benchmark mera kaam nahi naapta. *(5.3)*

**Madhav:** Aur kab mayne rakhta hai?

**Kabir:** Jab woh kuch aisa kar de jo pehle ho hi nahi sakta tha.

**Madhav:** Yaani kya badla?

**Kabir:** Sirf number nahi. Kaabiliyat ki kism.

**Madhav:** Teesra. Ek company kehti hai woh ek bada kaam kar payegi, agle saal.

**Kabir:** Woh andaza hai, jaankari nahi. Chapter 3.5. Kisi ko pata nahi ki agla model kya kar payega.

**Madhav:** Toh unhe kaise padhoge?

**Kabir:** Jo ho chuka hai use padhunga. Jo hoga uska daawa chhod dunga.

**Madhav:** Chautha. Aap kis cheez pe dhyaan doge jispe lagbhag koi charcha nahi hoti?

**Kabir:** Daam. Kyunki daam girne se naye kaam mumkin ho jaate hain.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Aur woh cheezein jo model ke bahar hain. Kyunki woh main kal laga sakta hoon.

**Madhav:** Bas. Ab aakhri sawal, aur yeh sabse zaroori hai. Aap kitna padhein?

**Kabir:** Jitna kaam ka ho.

**Madhav:** Aur kaam ka kya hai?

**Kabir:** Woh jo mere kaam ko badalta hai.

**Madhav:** Aur baaki?

**Kabir:** Baaki manoranjan hai. Aur woh theek hai, jab tak main use jaankari na samjhun.

### Naam

Chaar chhannan, kaam ke kram mein:

```
1.  GUNA YA PRATISHAT?
    das guna sasta, tez, ya bada    →  dekho
    20 percent behtar               →  chhodo

2.  NAYA NUMBER YA NAYI KISM?
    benchmark pe aage               →  chhodo
    aisa kaam jo pehle ho hi nahi sakta tha  →  dekho

3.  HO CHUKA HAI YA HOGA?
    "hamne yeh kiya"                →  padho
    "agle saal yeh hoga"            →  chhodo
                                       (kisi ko pata nahi, 3.5)

4.  MERE KAAM KO BADALTA HAI?
    haan                            →  padho aur aazmao
    nahi                            →  manoranjan hai, jaankari nahi
```

Aur teen cheezein jinpe sabse kam charcha hoti hai aur sabse zyada asar padta hai:

```
DAAM GIRNA        guna wala badlav, aur khabar mein sabse peeche
                  naye kaam mumkin ho jaate hain (Ch 7.1)

MODEL KE BAHAR    naye tools, behtar RAG, naye tareeke
                  aap kal laga sakte ho, kisi ke intezaar ke bina

WOH JO TOOT       kaunsa product band hua, kaunsa API mehnga hua
                  yeh batata hai ki kya sach mein kaam nahi kar raha
```

Teesri wali sabse kaam ki hai. **Kaamyabi ki khabar sabko milti hai. Nakaami ki khabar sirf unhe milti hai jo dhoondhte hain.** Aur nakaami zyada batati hai.

Ab ek aakhri baat, aur yeh iss kitaab ka poora point hai.

Aapko har cheez padhne ki zaroorat nahi hai, kyunki ab aapke paas ek naksha hai. Jab koi nayi cheez aati hai, toh aap use uski jagah pe rakh dete ho aur aage badh jaate ho.

**Aur agar koi cheez aisi aaye jiske liye aapke naksh e mein jagah na ho, toh woh sach mein nayi hai. Us pe rukna.**

Woh saal mein do-teen baar hota hai, aur baaki sab shor hai.

### Asli duniya se

2023 se 2026 tak, har hafte "AI badal gaya" wali khabarein aati rahin.

Ab peeche mud kar dekho: kitni cheezein sach mein badli?

Kuch hi. Baaki sab wahi transformer tha, thoda bada, thode behtar data pe, thodi alag dhalai ke saath, aur uske aas-paas kuch nayi parat.

Aur jo sach mein bade badlav the, unmein se kai ne khabar mein utni jagah nahi payi jitni ek naye benchmark number ne payi.

**Yeh iss chapter ka poora saboot hai: khabar ki maatra aur badlav ki maatra ka koi rishta nahi hai.**

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **charcha ki maatra ko asar samajhna.**

Jis cheez pe sabse zyada baat ho rahi hai, woh aksar sabse kam badalti hai. Aur jo cheez chup-chaap sabki neev badal rahi hai, uspe koi baat nahi karta.

Doosri galti: **FOMO se padhna.**

Log isliye padhte hain ki kahin peeche na reh jaayein. Aur woh ulta chalta hai: aap sau cheezein padh kar bhool jaate ho, aur ek cheez bhi aazmate nahi.

**Ek cheez aazmaana das cheezein padhne se zyada batata hai.** Kyunki aazmaane se aapko woh pata chalta hai jo kisi khabar mein nahi hoti: yeh aapke kaam mein kaisa chalta hai.

Teesri galti: **daawe aur nateeje ko ek samajhna.**

Ek company ka daawa ek marketing ka kaagaz hai. Uska matlab hai ki unhe kya bechna hai, na ki kya sach hai.

Aur unmein aksar jhooth nahi hota. Bas woh un cheezon ke baare mein chup rehte hain jo kaam nahi karti.

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Aaj do khabarein hain. "Naya model tark ke test mein 3 point aage." Aur "ek badi service ne apne daam aadhe kar diye." Kaunsi padhoge?

> **Jawab:** Doosri, bina soche.
>
> Teen point ek benchmark pe shor hai. Woh agle mahine palat sakta hai, aur aapke kaam se uska koi rishta nahi.
>
> Daam aadha hona ek asli badlav hai. Woh cheezein jo pehle mehngi hone ki wajah se nahi ban sakti thin, ab ban sakti hain.
>
> **Aur pehli wali ko das guna zyada charcha milegi.** Yeh iss chapter ka poora sabak hai.

**2. (samajh check)** Ek AI product ka demo dekha aur woh kamaal tha. Kya karoge?

> **Jawab:** Apne bees sawal chalao, apne asli kaam se. *(5.3)*
>
> Demo chune hue case dikhata hai. Woh jhooth nahi hai, aur woh aapke case ke baare mein kuch nahi kehta.
>
> Aur ek aur cheez dekho: **kya woh har kadam pe jaanch dikhata hai, ya sirf aakhri nateeja?** Agar sirf nateeja, toh us demo se aap yeh nahi jaan sakte ki beech mein kya hua. *(6.8)*

**3. (jodne wala)** Kitaab ke shuru mein Kabir ki shikayat thi ki har cheez "khabar" lagti hai. Ab woh kaise badla?

> **Jawab:** Ab har cheez ke liye ek jagah pehle se hai.
>
> Jab kuch naya aata hai, aap poochte ho: kaunsi parat, kaunsa dard, kaunsa sauda, andar ya bahar. Aur woh apni jagah pe baith jaata hai.
>
> Jo pehle "kuch naya" tha, woh ab "us purani cheez ka ek naya roop" hai.
>
> **Aur wahi poori kitaab ka maksad tha.** Aapne 100 cheezein yaad nahi ki. Aapne ek dhaancha banaya jismein nayi cheezein apne aap baith jaati hain.
>
> Aur jis din koi cheez nahi baithe, us din rukna. Woh sach mein nayi hai.

---

# Chapter 7.6  [SPINE]
## AI ko expert ki tarah chalana

*Iss chapter mein koi nayi cheez nahi hai. Har baat kitaab ke kisi chapter se aayi hai. Yeh sirf unhe kaam ke roop mein rakhta hai.*

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Ab aap sab jaante ho. Toh batao: expert aur aam user mein farak kya hai?

**Kabir:** Expert ko pata hai ki andar kya ho raha hai.

**Madhav:** Aur us jaankari se woh kya alag karta hai?

**Kabir:** Woh use aisi cheez nahi poochta jo woh kar hi nahi sakta.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Woh use jaankari deta hai, yaad se maangta nahi.

**Madhav:** Aur?

**Kabir:** Woh jawab jaanchta hai.

**Madhav:** Bas teen? Ek aur socho. Aap ek sawal likhte ho. Usmein aapka nazariya hota hai?

**Kabir:** Aksar hota hai. "Mujhe lagta hai X sahi hai..."

**Madhav:** Aur uska kya asar hai?

**Kabir:** Woh us taraf khinch jaata hai. Chapter 2.8, 5.4.

**Madhav:** Toh chautha?

**Kabir:** Apna jawab sawal mein mat daalo.

**Madhav:** Aur paanchva. Ek lambi baat-cheet mein jawab kharaab hote ja rahe hain. Expert kya karega?

**Kabir:** Nayi chat shuru karega. Chapter 4.4.

**Madhav:** Aur chhatha, jo sabse kam kiya jaata hai. Aap ek bada kaam dete ho. Kya karna chahiye?

**Kabir:** Chhote tukdon mein todna. Kyunki lambe kaam mein galtiyan judti hain. Chapter 6.4.

**Madhav:** Aur har tukde ke baad?

**Kabir:** Dekh lena ki theek hai ya nahi, aage badhne se pehle.

**Madhav:** Bas. Ab dhyaan do ki inmein se ek bhi "jaadui shabd" nahi hai.

**Kabir:** Sab kuch iss baat se aaya ki andar kya ho raha hai.

**Madhav:** Aur wahi asli farak hai.

### Aath niyam

**1. Use woh mat poochho jo woh kar hi nahi sakta.**

Pehle poochho: yeh aankh ki seema hai ya andar ki? *(5.2)*

```
akshar ginna, hisaab, taazi baat, aapka data
   →  aankh ki seema, tool ya jaankari se hat jaayegi

"kya tumhe pakka pata hai," "apni galti dhoondho"
   →  andar ki seema, ismein kuch nahi milega
```

**2. Jaankari do, yaad se mat maango.**

Jo cheez data mein kam thi, wahan woh sabse zyada aatmvishwas se galat hoga *(5.1)*. Toh use saamne rakh do.

```
kamzor    "hamari chhutti ki policy kya hai?"
mazboot   "yeh rahi hamari policy [text]. Iske hisaab se batao..."
```

**3. Apna jawab sawal mein mat daalo.**

```
kamzor    "mujhe lagta hai X sahi hai, kya main sahi hoon?"
kamzor    "kya yeh sach hai ki X?"
mazboot   "X ke paksh aur vipaksh mein sabse mazboot tark kya hain?"
mazboot   "yeh galat kaise ho sakta hai?"
```

**4. Sandarbh se matlab kheencho.**

Ek shabd ka matlab uske aas-paas se banta hai *(3.2)*. Toh pehle duniya set karo, phir sawal poochho.

```
kamzor    "python ke baare mein batao"
mazboot   "main ek web server bana raha hoon. Python ke baare mein..."
```

**5. Bada kaam todo. Har tukde ke baad dekho.**

Lambe loop mein galtiyan judti hain aur pata nahi chalta *(6.4)*. Teen kadam ka kaam tees kadam ke kaam se lagbhag hamesha behtar chalta hai.

**6. Jaanch hamesha bahar se.**

Code chala kar, source dekh kar, hisaab milaa kar. Model se uski hi jaanch mat karwao *(5.4)*.

**7. Bharosa naapna ho toh sthirta naapo.**

Wahi sawal teen baar, alag chats mein. Ek jaise jawab ek sanket hain, alag alag jawab ek chetavni *(4.3)*.

**8. Chat gandi ho jaaye toh nayi shuru karo.**

Purani baat naye shabdon ka matlab kheenchti hai, context bharta hai, aur shuruaat kat jaati hai *(4.4)*. Nayi chat sabse sasta ilaaj hai.

### Chhoti baatein jo bada farak karti hain

```
JAWAB KI SHAKAL MAANGO      "sirf number do," "table mein do"
                             kyunki lamba jawab mehnga hai (4.2)
                             aur jaanchna mushkil (5.4)

USE ROLE MAT DO, SANDARBH DO "tum ek expert ho" se kam faayda
                             "yeh rahi asli jaankari" se zyada

KAM TOOLS DO                 zyada tools matlab galat chunav aur
                             zyada daam (6.3)

MISAAL DO                    ek achha udaharan das nirdeshon se
                             behtar kaam karta hai

NA KARNE WALI CHEEZ LIKHO    "yeh mat karna" aksar "yeh karna" se
                             zyada kaam ka hota hai
```

### Asli duniya se

Do log ek hi model istemaal karte hain. Ek kehta hai woh bekaar hai. Doosra usse roz asli kaam nikalta hai.

Farak aksar in paanch cheezon mein hota hai:

```
ek yaad se poochta hai        doosra jaankari deta hai
ek bada kaam ek saath deta    doosra tukdon mein
ek jawab maan leta hai        doosra jaanchta hai
ek apna jawab sawal mein daal doosra khula sawal poochta hai
ek ek hi lambi chat mein rehta doosra nayi shuru karta hai
```

**Model dono ke liye ek hi hai.** Farak iss baat mein hai ki kisko pata hai ki andar kya ho raha hai.

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **"prompt engineering" ko jaadui shabdon ki list samajhna.**

Log lambi templates copy karte hain jinme "tum ek vishwa-prasiddh expert ho" jaisa likha hota hai.

Uska asar chhota hai. Bada asar in cheezon se aata hai: kaunsi jaankari saath gayi, kaam kitna chhota tha, aur jaanch kya thi.

Doosri galti: **AI ko convince karne ki koshish.**

Log bahas karte hain, dobara samjhaate hain, gussa karte hain. Woh sab context mein jud jaata hai aur aksar cheezein aur bigaad deta hai.

Agar do koshish mein sahi nahi hua, toh **nayi chat, behtar sawal.** Bahas se kuch nahi milta.

Teesri galti: **uske aatmvishwas ko sanket samajhna.** Ab tak yeh kitaab yeh baat paanch baar keh chuki hai, aur woh isliye ki yeh sabse mehngi galti hai.

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Aap ek kaam do baar poochte ho aur dono baar jawab kharaab hai. Ab kya?

> **Jawab:** Teesri baar wahi mat poochho.
>
> Poochho: kya use woh jaankari mili jo chahiye thi? Kya kaam bahut bada tha? Kya maine apna jawab sawal mein daal diya? Kya chat gandi ho chuki hai?
>
> Aur phir **nayi chat, badla hua sawal.**
>
> Usi chat mein teesri koshish lagbhag hamesha kharaab hoti hai, kyunki ab do kharaab jawab bhi context mein hain aur woh agle jawab ko kheench rahe hain.

**2. (samajh check)** Aapko ek lambe document se das cheezein nikaalni hain. Kaise poochoge?

> **Jawab:** Ek ek karke, ya chhote batch mein. Sab ek saath nahi.
>
> Kyunki das cheezein ek saath maangne mein woh kuch chhod dega aur aapko pata nahi chalega. Aur jawab lamba hoga, jo mehnga aur jaanchne mein mushkil hai.
>
> Aur jawab aisi shakal mein maango jo jaanchi ja sake: har cheez ke saath woh line jahan se woh aayi.
>
> **Phir aap ek nazar mein dekh sakte ho ki woh line sach mein document mein hai ya nahi.** Yeh Chapter 6.2 ka wahi source dikhane wala niyam hai, ab aapke haath mein.

**3. (jodne wala)** In aath niyamon mein se kaunsa Book 1 se aata hai, AI se nahi?

> **Jawab:** "Bada kaam todo aur har tukde ke baad dekho."
>
> Woh Book 1 Chapter 8.4 hai: thoda badlo, dekho, phir aage badho. Aur Chapter 7.5: chhota kaam theek se aanka jaata hai, bada nahi.
>
> Aur "jaanch hamesha bahar se" bhi Book 1 Chapter 7.2 se aata hai: jaanch ka kaam bharosa dena hai, aur woh cheez khud ko nahi jaanch sakti.
>
> **Yeh dhyaan dene laayak hai. AI ko chalane ke aadhe niyam AI ke baare mein hain hi nahi.** Woh us baat ke baare mein hain ki kisi bhi cheez pe kaise kaam kiya jaata hai jispe poora bharosa na ho.

---

# Chapter 7.7  [SPINE]
## Kisi bhi nayi cheez ko khud derive kaise karein

### Samvaad

**Madhav:** Do kitaabein khatam hone wali hain. Ek aakhri cheez deni hai, aur woh technology ki nahi hai. Batao ki iss poori kitaab mein humne kya kiya?

**Kabir:** Aapne sawal poochhe. Maine jawab nikaale.

**Madhav:** Kaunsa tareeka baar baar chala?

**Kabir:** Aap pehle problem dete the. Phir poochte the ki main kya karta.

**Madhav:** Aur naam kab aata tha?

**Kabir:** Aakhir mein. Jab cheez ban chuki hoti thi.

**Madhav:** Kyun aakhir mein?

**Kabir:** Kyunki naam pehle aata toh main use yaad karta. Aakhir mein aaya toh woh us cheez ka label ban gaya jo maine banayi thi.

**Madhav:** Ab ek aur cheez. Har baar jab aap ek jawab nikaal lete the, main kya poochta tha?

**Kabir:** "Aur uski keemat kya hai."

**Madhav:** Kyun?

**Kabir:** Kyunki kuch bhi muft nahi milta.

**Madhav:** Aur teesra? Jab aap koi jawab nikaal lete the?

**Kabir:** Aap use todne ki koshish karte the.

**Madhav:** Ab yeh sochiye: kya yeh sirf technology pe chalta hai?

**Kabir:** Nahi. Yeh kisi bhi cheez pe chalega.

**Madhav:** Chalo aazmate hain. Aap ek naye field mein ho jismein aapko kuch nahi pata. Pehla kadam?

**Kabir:** Yeh poochna ki yeh field kis dard ke liye hai.

**Madhav:** Kaise poochoge, jab aapko kuch nahi pata?

**Kabir:** Yeh poochunga ki iske bina log kya karte the.

**Madhav:** Yeh sabse achha sawal hai jo kisi naye field mein poocha ja sakta hai. Doosra kadam?

**Kabir:** Yeh dekhna ki sabse neeche kya hai. Woh cheez jispe baaki sab khada hai.

**Madhav:** Aur agar woh na dikhe?

**Kabir:** Toh poochunga: jab yeh toot ta hai toh kya hota hai. Book 1 Chapter 0.4.

**Madhav:** Teesra?

**Kabir:** Har cheez ka sauda dhoondhna. Kya deti hai, kya leti hai.

**Madhav:** Aur chautha?

**Kabir:** Apne banaye hue jawab ko todne ki koshish karna.

**Madhav:** Aur paanchva, jo iss kitaab mein har part ke aakhir mein tha?

**Kabir:** Naksha banana. Taaki nayi cheezein us par baith sakein.

**Madhav:** Bas. Yeh paanch cheezein hi asli auzaar hain. Baaki sab jo aapne seekha, woh inhi se nikla.

### Paanch kadam

**1. Poocho: iske bina log kya karte the?**

Har cheez kisi dard ke liye bani thi. Agar aap dard nahi dhoondh sakte, toh aap cheez samajh nahi sakte, sirf yaad kar sakte ho.

Aur agar dard mila hi nahi, toh do mein se ek hai: aapne dhoondha nahi, ya wahan dard tha hi nahi aur woh cheez marketing hai.

**2. Sabse neeche kya hai?**

Har cheez kisi parat pe khadi hai. Aur har parat neeche wali ko chhupa deti hai *(Book 1, Ch 0.4)*.

Agar neeche wali parat na dikhe, toh ek hi sawal poochho: **jab yeh toot ta hai toh kya dikhta hai?** Woh hamesha andar ka hissa dikha deta hai.

**3. Har cheez ka sauda kya hai?**

Jo cheez kuch deti hai, woh kuch leti hai. Agar nahi dikh raha toh aapne dhoondha nahi.

```
index    →  padhna tez, likhna dheema
cache    →  tez, purana ho sakta hai
attention→  doori se farak nahi, kaam varg mein
RAG      →  taazi jaankari, aur dhoondhna kamzor ho toh sab kharaab
tools    →  zyada kaam, zyada khatra
```

**4. Apne jawab ko todo.**

Jab aap kisi nateeje pe pahunch jao, toh use maano mat. Use todne ki koshish karo.

Book 1 Chapter 6.9 mein humne apna hi daawa chaar baar todne ki koshish ki thi. Woh bacha, aur usi se woh mazboot hua.

**Jo daawa hamla nahi jhel chuka, woh sirf ek raay hai.**

**5. Naksha banao, list nahi.**

Yeh sabse zaroori hai. Ek list mein sau cheezein hoti hain aur aap unhe bhool jaate ho.

Ek naksha mein jagahein hoti hain. Aur nayi cheez ko yaad nahi karna padta, use bas rakhna padta hai.

**Aur jis din koi cheez kisi jagah pe na baithe, us din rukna. Woh sach mein nayi hai.**

### Asli duniya se

Yeh tareeka nahi banaya gaya. Woh baar baar dobara khoja gaya hai, alag alag jagahon pe, alag naamon se.

Woh hai: cheez ko maan mat lo, use pehle sidhaanton se dobara bana kar dekho.

Aur woh dheema lagta hai. Woh dheema hai, shuruaat mein.

Uska poora faayda uss din milta hai jab kuch naya aata hai. Jisne yaad kiya tha, use naya kuch bhi ek nayi cheez lagti hai. Jisne dhaancha banaya, use woh ek jaani-pehchani cheez ka naya roop lagta hai.

**Aur woh farak har saal badhta hai.**

### Yahan log kya galat samajhte hain

Sabse aam galti: **yeh sochna ki yeh sirf technical logon ke liye hai.**

Yeh paanch sawal kisi bhi cheez pe lagte hain: kanoon, dawa, paisa, sarkar, kisi bhi kaarobar pe.

Book 1 Part 0 isi ka saboot tha. Wahan humne technology se pehle paisa, value aur society ko isi tareeke se banaya tha.

Doosri galti: **yeh sochna ki dhaancha banana zyada waqt leta hai.**

Woh shuru mein zyada leta hai aur uske baad har cheez sasti hoti jaati hai *(Book 1, Ch 0.5)*.

Ek saal baad tukde wale ke paas sau alag cheezein hoti hain, jinmein se aadhi bhool chuki hoti hain. Dhaanche wale ke paas ek cheez hoti hai jismein sau jagah hain.

Teesri galti, aur yeh sabse gehri hai: **jawab ko yaad rakhna aur tareeke ko bhool jaana.**

Iss kitaab ke jawab purane ho jaayenge. Book 2 ka bahut kuch do saal mein badal jaayega.

**Yeh paanch kadam purane nahi honge.** Woh hi asli cheez hai jo aapko di ja rahi hai.

### Sochne ke liye

**1. (samajh check)** Aap ek bilkul naye field mein ho: maan lo dawa banane ka udyog. Pehle teen sawal kya poochoge?

> **Jawab:**
>
> **Ek:** iske bina log kya karte the? Yaani ek nayi dawa aane se pehle log kya karte the, aur woh kyun kaafi nahi tha.
>
> **Do:** sabse neeche kya hai? Yaani woh cheez jispe poora udyog khada hai. Shayad jaanch ka tareeka, ya kanoon, ya woh khoj jo ek baar hoti hai aur saalon chalti hai.
>
> **Teen:** har cheez ka sauda kya hai? Tez manzoori aur suraksha ke beech. Daam aur pahunch ke beech.
>
> **Aur dhyaan do: aapne dawa ke baare mein kuch nahi jaana. Aapne yeh jaana ki kya poochna hai.** Aur woh hi asli shuruaat hai.

**2. (samajh check)** Koi aapko ek nayi cheez batata hai aur aap uska dard nahi dhoondh paate. Kya nikalta hai?

> **Jawab:** Do mein se ek.
>
> **Ek:** aapne poori tarah nahi dhoondha. Ek aur sawal poochho: kaun iske liye paisa de raha hai, aur kyun?
>
> **Do:** wahan dard hai hi nahi, aur woh cheez kisi aur wajah se maujood hai. Aksar woh wajah yeh hoti hai ki koi use bech raha hai.
>
> **Yeh sawal ek bahut achha filter hai**, aur woh technology se bahar bhi utna hi kaam karta hai.

**3. (jodne wala)** Do kitaabon mein sabse zyada baar kaunsi baat dohrayi gayi?

> **Jawab:** Shayad yeh: **har cheez ka ek sauda hai, aur agar woh nahi dikh raha toh aapne dhoondha nahi.**
>
> Woh index mein tha, cache mein, taale mein, container mein, attention mein, context mein, tools mein.
>
> Aur uske bilkul paas ek doosri hai: **har cheez kisi dard ka jawab hai.**
>
> Yeh do saath mein ek poora auzaar bante hain. Kisi bhi cheez ke saamne khade ho jao aur do sawal poochho: **yeh kis dard ke liye hai, aur iski keemat kya hai?**
>
> Iske aage lagbhag kuch nahi bachta.

---

# Chapter 7.8  [SPINE]
## Aakhri test

*Yeh dono kitaabon par hai. Har sawal ka jawab neeche hai, lekin pehle khud socho. Agar aadhe se zyada aa gaye, toh kitaabon ne apna kaam kar diya.*

### Paanch khabarein

Neeche paanch khabarein hain. Har ek pe chaar sawal chalao:

```
1.  yeh kaunsi parat pe hai?
2.  yeh kis dard ke liye hai?
3.  iska sauda kya hai?
4.  yeh model ke andar hai ya bahar?
```

---

**Khabar 1.** "Ek nayi company kehti hai ki uska AI aapke saare company documents padh kar kisi bhi sawal ka jawab de sakta hai."

> **Jawab:** Yeh RAG hai *(6.2)*, model ke **bahar**.
>
> **Dard:** documents bahut hain, sab context mein nahi aa sakte, aur model unhe jaanta nahi.
>
> **Sauda:** poora dhoondhne pe khada hai. Agar dhoondhna kamzor hai toh model ke paas jawab hoga hi nahi, aur woh kuch bana dega *(5.1)*.
>
> **Poochne wale sawal:** tukde kitne bade hain? Jawab ke saath source dikhta hai? Aur agar jawab documents mein hai hi nahi, toh woh mana karta hai ya kuch bana deta hai?

---

**Khabar 2.** "Naye model ka context 10 lakh token ho gaya hai, isliye ab RAG ki zaroorat nahi."

> **Jawab:** Doosra hissa galat hai.
>
> **Parat:** inference aur architecture, model ke **andar**.
>
> **Sauda teen tarah ka:** har sawal pe poora prefill, isliye mehnga aur dheema *(4.2)*. Beech ka hissa dab jaata hai *(4.4)*. Aur attention ka kaam varg mein badhta hai *(3.3)*.
>
> **Toh RAG khatam nahi hua.** Chun kar bhejna abhi bhi sasta, tez aur zyada bharosemand hai. Bada context ek suvidha hai, hal nahi.

---

**Khabar 3.** "Ek AI agent ne apne aap ek poora software project banaya, shuruaat se ant tak."

> **Jawab:** Yeh loop hai *(6.4)*, model ke **bahar**.
>
> **Sabse pehla sawal:** har kadam pe jaanch kya thi?
>
> Agar code chal raha tha aur test paas ho rahe the, toh yeh sach ho sakta hai, kyunki code un kaamon mein hai jinme har kadam bahar se jaancha ja sakta hai *(6.8)*.
>
> Aur jo nahi bataya gaya: kitni baar bhatka, kitne kadam lage, kitna kharcha hua, aur kya woh project sach mein kisi kaam ka tha *(Book 1, Ch 8.2)*.

---

**Khabar 4.** "AI ka daam pichhle saal se paanch guna gir gaya."

> **Jawab:** Yeh paanchon khabaron mein **sabse badi** hai, aur ise sabse kam charcha milegi.
>
> **Parat:** inference *(4.1)*.
>
> **Kyun badi hai:** guna wala badlav hai, pratishat wala nahi *(Book 1, Ch 2.2)*. Woh cheezein jo pehle mehngi hone ki wajah se ban hi nahi sakti thin, ab ban sakti hain.
>
> **Aur uska doosra hissa:** woh aapke mukable ke liye bhi sasti hui *(7.1)*. Toh yeh mauka bhi hai aur khatra bhi.

---

**Khabar 5.** "Ek badi company kehti hai ki agle saal AI zyadatar naukriyan kar lega."

> **Jawab:** Yeh khabar nahi hai. Yeh ek andaza hai *(3.5)*.
>
> **Kisi ko nahi pata ki agla model kya kar payega**, un companies ko bhi nahi. Woh train karte hain aur phir dekhte hain.
>
> Aur woh company yeh keh kar kuch bech rahi hai: apna product, apne shares, ya apni jagah.
>
> **Padhne laayak hissa:** unhone ab tak kya kiya. Chhod dene laayak hissa: unhone kya hoga bataya.

### Paanch aur sawal, dono kitaabon se

**1.** Aapka AI product dheema hai. Paanch alag wajahein batao, alag alag parat se.

> **Jawab:**
>
> **Network:** doori aur latency *(Book 1, Ch 4.3, 6.7)*.
>
> **Line:** vyast waqt mein batch ka intezaar *(4.1)*, aur seema paar ho toh shrinkhla *(Book 1, Ch 6.6)*.
>
> **Prefill:** sawal ya sandarbh bada hai *(4.2)*.
>
> **Decode:** jawab lamba hai *(4.2)*.
>
> **Aapka apna code:** RAG mein dhoondhna dheema hai, ya N+1 jaisa kuch *(Book 1, Ch 6.7)*.
>
> Aur zyadatar log sirf aakhri do dekhte hain.

**2.** Aapka AI kabhi kabhi customer ko galat jaankari de deta hai. Teen alag ilaaj batao, sabse kamzor se sabse mazboot.

> **Jawab:**
>
> **Kamzor:** nirdesh mein likh do ki galat jaankari mat do. Yeh sirf sambhavna badalta hai.
>
> **Beech ka:** jaankari saath bhejo aur kaho ki sirf usi se jawab do *(6.2)*.
>
> **Mazboot:** jawab ko bahar se jaancho, model ke bahar. Kya jo source usne diya woh sach mein bheje gaye tukdon mein hai? Nahi toh jawab rok do *(5.4, 6.2)*.
>
> Aur usse bhi mazboot: aise jawab customer tak jaane hi mat do bina insaan ke *(6.3)*.

**3.** Ek dost kehta hai "maine AI ko apne data pe train kiya." Do sambhavnayein batao.

> **Jawab:**
>
> **Ek, aur zyada sambhavit:** usne RAG banaya hai. Kuch train nahi hua. Ek dhoondhne wali cheez hai jo har sawal pe tukde bhejti hai *(6.2)*.
>
> **Do:** usne sach mein ek model ko apne data pe dobara dhaala hai. Yeh mumkin hai, mehnga hai, aur uske apne khatre hain: purana gyaan kamzor ho sakta hai, aur data badalne pe dobara karna padta hai.
>
> **Farak zaroori hai:** pehle mein document badlo toh agla jawab turant naya. Doosre mein poora kaam dobara.

**4.** Aap ek AI product bana rahe ho. Chaar saal baad bhi aapke paas kya bacha rahega?

> **Jawab:** Woh nahi jo aapne banaya. Woh jo jama hua.
>
> Model badal jaayega, sasta ho jaayega, aur jo aap bana rahe ho use koi bhi dobara bana lega *(7.2, 7.3)*.
>
> Jo bachega: aapka apna data, aapke log, aur woh samajh jo us kaam ke andar rehne se aayi.
>
> **Aur agar aapka poora mol kisi aur ke model pe khada hai, toh kuch nahi bachega** *(Book 1, Ch 6.4; 7.3)*.

**5.** Iss cheez ki sabse gehri seema kya hai, aur kya woh hategi?

> **Jawab:** Woh nahi jaanta ki use kya nahi pata, aur woh apni galti khud nahi pehchan sakta *(5.2)*.
>
> Aankh ki seemayein tezi se hat rahi hain: hisaab, taazi jaankari, aapka data, akshar. Ye do lagbhag nahi hilee hain.
>
> **Aur unhi do se lagbhag har asli khatra aata hai:** hallucination, agent ka bhatakna, aur woh jhoothi jaanch jo jaanch jaisi lagti hai.
>
> Kya woh hatengi, yeh main nahi jaanta. Aur jo koi aapko poore vishwas se jawab de, uspe shak karna.

### Aur ab

Do kitaabein khatam.

Book 1 mein aapne ek switch se shuru karke ek poora product khola. Book 2 mein aapne ek file se shuru karke ek agent khola.

Aur ab aapke paas woh cheez hai jo shuruaat mein nahi thi: **ek naksha, jismein nayi cheezein apne aap baith jaati hain.**

Do baatein aakhir mein.

**Pehli:** Book 2 ka kuch hissa do saal mein purana ho jaayega. Model ke naam, unki kaabiliyat, unka daam, aur woh seemayein jo aaj sakht hain. Woh badlega.

Jo nahi badlega woh yeh hai: probability, seekhne ka tareeka, attention ka idea, agent ka loop, aur woh chaar sawal jo kisi bhi nayi cheez pe chalte hain.

**Doosri:** aapne 100 cheezein yaad nahi ki. Aapne unhe banaya.

Aur jo aapne khud banaya hai, woh purana nahi hota. Woh sirf badhta hai.

Chapter 7.7 ke paanch kadam aapke paas hain. Agli baar jab kuch aisa aaye jiske liye aapke naksh e mein jagah na ho, toh aap use khud bana loge, bina kisi kitaab ke.

**Aur wahi shuru se maksad tha.**

---

# Glossary

Book 2 ke shabd, aur woh chapter jahan aapne unhe kamaya. Book 1 ke shabd uski apni glossary mein hain.

---

**agent** (6.4) — ek model, kuch tools, aur ek dohrav. Iske alaawa kuch nahi. Woh har kadam pe agla sambhavit kadam chunta hai.

**agent loop** (6.4) — karo, nateeja dekho, phir tay karo, dohrao. Context har kadam pe bharta hai aur galtiyan judti jaati hain.

**attention** (3.3) — har shabd har doosre shabd ko dekhta hai, har jodi ka ek wazan nikalta hai, aur nayi jagah sabka mishran hoti hai. Doori se farak nahi padta, ginti ke varg se padta hai.

**batching** (4.1) — kai sawal ek saath chalana, taaki model ki file ek hi baar memory se aaye. Isi se daam gir ta hai, aur isi se vyast waqt mein jawab dheema lagta hai.

**benchmark** (5.3) — tay-shuda test. Teen kamzoriyan: woh training data mein aa jaata hai, uske liye taiyaari ki jaati hai, aur uska aapke kaam se koi rishta nahi hai.

**BPE** (1.6) — tokens banane ka tareeka: baar baar sabse aam jodi ko milaate jao. Isi se tay hota hai ki kaunsi bhasha sasti hai.

**calibrated** (1.3) — jab "70 percent" ka matlab sach mein yeh ho ki aise sau mein se lagbhag 70 baar hota hai. Mausam ke andaaze aksar calibrated hote hain. AI models aksar nahi.

**context window** (4.4) — kitna text ek baar mein diya ja sakta hai. Do seemayein isse banti hain: attention ka kaam varg mein, aur KV cache ki memory.

**cutoff** (2.6) — woh tarikh jiske baad ki duniya model ko pata nahi hai.

**decode** (4.2) — jawab banane ka hissa. Ek-ek token karke, aur har token pe poori file memory se aati hai. Isliye output input se mehnga hai.

**embedding** (3.1) — kisi cheez ke liye numbers ki ek lambi list, jo ek badi khali jagah mein uski jagah batati hai. Doori ka matlab rishta.

**emergence** (3.5) — kuch kaabiliyat chhote models mein nahi hoti aur ek paimane ke baad dikhne lagti hai. Kya woh sach mein chhalang hai, ispe bahas hai.

**hallucination** (5.1) — cheezein bana lena. Teen wajahein: uska kaam hi agla token dena hai, usmein khaali jagah nahi dikhti, aur use aatmvishwas ke liye dhaala gaya. Kam ho sakta hai, khatam nahi.

**inference** (4.1) — model ka istemaal. Training se alag aur bahut sasta. Aur ek chalte product mein kul kharcha training se bada ho jaata hai.

**KV cache** (4.1) — pichhle tokens ka bacha hua kaam. Iske bina lamba jawab varg mein mehnga hota. Iski keemat memory hai.

**layer** (3.3) — attention aur uske baad ka kaam, ek baar. Bade models mein sau se zyada baar dohraya jaata hai.

**learning rate** (2.5) — dhalaan pe kitna bada kadam. Bahut bada toh sab bikhar jaata hai, bahut chhota toh training kabhi khatam nahi hoti.

**loss** (2.4) — ek number jo batata hai ki model kitna galat hai. Poori training ka lakshya use girana hai. Woh sach nahi naapta.

**lost in the middle** (4.4) — bade context mein beech ka hissa aksar dab jaata hai. Context mein hona aur istemaal hona alag baatein hain.

**model** (2.9) — do cheezein: ek sthir file jismein numbers hain, aur use chalane wala code. Jo aap istemaal karte ho woh ek poora dher hai, aur uska bada hissa model ke bahar hai.

**model collapse** (2.7) — jab har agli peedhi pichhli ke likhe pe train hoti hai aur dheere dheere kamzor hoti jaati hai.

**multi-head** (3.3) — attention kai baar, alag alag tarah se, taaki alag kism ke rishte ek saath pakde ja sakein.

**multimodal** (3.6) — ek hi model jo text, photo, awaaz aur video sambhale. Sab tokens mein todi jaati hain, aur unka daam unke token count se aata hai.

**overfitting** (2.4) — model apne data ko ratt leta hai aur naye pe kamzor rehta hai. Isliye thodi galti rehna zaroori hai.

**parameters** (2.3) — weights ki ginti. "70 billion parameters" matlab 70 arab numbers. Ab yeh woh naap nahi raha jo do saal pehle tha.

**prefill** (4.2) — aapka sawal padhne ka hissa. Poora ek saath sambhala jaata hai, aur isi mein "pehla shabd" ka waqt jaata hai.

**prompt injection** (6.7) — bahar ki kisi cheez mein nirdesh chhupa dena, jise model padh kar maan leta hai. Iska koi poora hal abhi nahi hai. Asli bachaav: tools kam, daayra chhota.

**RAG** (6.2) — pehle dhoondho, phir jawab do. Model ko sikhaana nahi, use dikhana. Jab jawab kharaab aaye toh problem aksar dhoondhne mein hoti hai, model mein nahi.

**RLHF** (2.8) — insaan ki pasand se model ko dhaalna. Yahin se "sahayak" wali shakal aati hai, aur yahin se aatmvishwas aur chaploosi bhi.

**scaling laws** (3.5) — hisaab jo batata hai ki kitna kharch karne se loss kitna girega. Kharcha andaza laga sakte ho, kaabiliyat nahi.

**sycophancy** (2.8) — chaploosi. Model aapse zyada sehmat hota hai kyunki insaanon ne aise jawab chune the.

**temperature** (4.3) — chunav mein kitna random. Zero ka matlab "sahi" nahi, "dohraya ja sakne wala" hai.

**token** (1.5) — text ka ek tukda, model ke liye ek number. Akshar se bada, shabd se chhota. Model ko akshar dikhte hi nahi.

**tool call** (6.3) — model ka woh likha hua jo ek tool maangta hai. Model kuch karta nahi, woh maangta hai, aur ek program karta hai.

**training** (2.2) — agla token chhupao, guess karwao, galti naapo, numbers hilao, kharabon baar. Jawab data mein pehle se hota hai, isi chaalaki ne paimana mumkin banaya.

**transformer** (3.4) — poora design: tokens, jagah, kram, sau layers, aur aakhir mein sambhavna. 2017 se lagbhag wahi hai.

**vector database** (6.2) — woh database jo embedding se "paas wali" cheez dhoondhta hai. Book 1 ke index ka hi ek roop.

**weights** (2.3) — model ke andar ke numbers. Ek nichod, ek copy nahi. Ek weight ka koi matlab nahi hota.

---

# Kahan dhoondhein, aur poora naksha

## Roz ke sawal

```
"AI ne mujhe galat jawab diya, aatmvishwas se"
   →  5.1  teen wajahein
   →  2.6  us baat ka data kam tha
   →  5.4  ab kya karna hai

"lambi chat mein jawab kharaab hote ja rahe hain"
   →  4.4  context bhar gaya, shuruaat kat gayi
   →  3.2  purani baat naye shabdon ka matlab kheench rahi hai
   →  ilaaj: nayi chat

"ek hi sawal, do alag jawab"
   →  4.3  temperature

"pehla shabd der se aata hai"
   →  4.2  prefill. Sandarbh chhota karo.

"jawab dheere dheere aa raha hai"
   →  4.2  decode. Chhota jawab maango.
   →  4.1  ya bojh zyada hai

"woh akshar nahi gin pata"
   →  1.5  use akshar dikhte hi nahi
   →  ilaaj: akshar alag alag karke do

"main jo type karta hoon uska kya hota hai"
   →  6.6  teen alag sawal, aur poora sandarbh jaata hai
```

## Kuch banane ke sawal

```
"apne documents pe AI kaise lagayein"
   →  6.2  RAG, aur uski teen kamzoriyan

"jawab galat aa raha hai, model badlun?"
   →  6.2  pehle dekho kaunse tukde bheje gaye
   →  5.3  phir apne bees sawal chalao

"agent ko kaunse tools doon"
   →  6.3  padhna aasani se, badalna soch kar, mitana kabhi nahi
   →  6.7  aur bahar ka data padhna + bhejna, dono saath sabse khatarnak

"kaunsa model chunun"
   →  5.3  apna chhota test banao. Benchmark kuch nahi batata.

"context bada lun ya chun kar bhejun"
   →  4.4  bada context suvidha hai, hal nahi
   →  6.2  chun kar bhejna sasta, tez, zyada bharosemand

"kya main iss AI product pe apna business bana sakta hoon"
   →  7.3  kiski naali hai
   →  7.1  paisa kahan ruk raha hai
```

## Khabar samajhne ke sawal

```
"yeh khabar mayne rakhti hai?"
   →  7.5  chaar chhannan
   →  7.4  chaar sawal

"kya yeh sach mein naya hai?"
   →  3.4  design 2017 se wahi hai
   →  7.4  model ke andar hai ya bahar

"AI kitna aage jaayega?"
   →  3.5  kisi ko nahi pata. Kharcha andaza laga sakte ho,
           kaabiliyat nahi.
```

## Paisa aur career ke sawal

```
"AI industry mein paisa kaun kama raha hai"        →  7.1
"ek aadmi ab kya bana sakta hai"                   →  7.2
"leverage ab kahan hai"                            →  7.3
"AI se kaam kaise nikalun"                         →  7.6
"kisi bhi nayi cheez ko khud kaise samjhun"        →  7.7
```

## Poora naksha, ek page

```
╔══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╗
║  BOOK 2   THE THINKING MACHINE                               ║
╠══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╣
║                                                              ║
║  PRODUCT      app, filter, sandarbh, tools                    ║
║               ── AI ka bada hissa yahan hai                   ║
║  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  ║
║  AGENT        tools + loop                                    ║
║               model ke paas koi taakat nahi                   ║
║               nirdesh aur data mein farak nahi kar sakta      ║
║  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  ║
║  RAG          pehle dhoondho, phir jawab do                   ║
║               embedding se, matlab se                         ║
║  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  ║
║  INFERENCE    prefill (ek saath)  |  decode (ek-ek karke)     ║
║               chunav, temperature, context ki seema           ║
║  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  ║
║  ARCHITECTURE token → jagah → attention → x100 layers         ║
║               doori se farak nahi, ginti ke varg se           ║
║  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  ║
║  TRAINING     data → loss → dhalaan → weights                 ║
║               phir insaan ki pasand se dhalai                 ║
║  ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────  ║
║  BOOK 1       server, RAM, GPU, network, database,            ║
║               cache, kharcha, git, protocol                   ║
╚══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════╝


  SAAT BAATEIN JO POORI KITAAB MEIN DOHRAYI GAYIN

  1.  Model ke andar teesri kism ke numbers hain:
      kisi ne likhe nahi, ek process se bane.

  2.  Sabse achha jawab bhi aksar galat hoga.
      Woh us tareeke ke andar hai, bug nahi.

  3.  Jo aap naapte ho wahi milta hai.
      Loss ne agla token naapa. Sach kabhi naapa hi nahi gaya.

  4.  Matlab kisi ne daala nahi. Woh side effect hai.

  5.  Do kism ki seemayein hain.
      Aankh ki: tools se hat ti hain, tezi se hat rahi hain.
      Andar ki: insaan chahiye, aur woh lagbhag nahi hilee.

  6.  Har asli jaanch model ke BAHAR se aati hai.
      Ek cheez khud ko nahi jaanch sakti.

  7.  Har cheez ka ek sauda hai.
      Agar nahi dikh raha toh aapne dhoondha nahi.


  KISI BHI NAYI CHEEZ PE CHAAR SAWAL

     1.  Yeh kaunsi parat pe hai?
     2.  Yeh kis dard ke liye bani thi?
     3.  Iska sauda kya hai?
     4.  Yeh model ke andar hai ya bahar?


  KISI BHI NAYE FIELD PE PAANCH KADAM        (7.7)

     1.  Iske bina log kya karte the?
     2.  Sabse neeche kya hai?
             (na dikhe toh: jab yeh toot ta hai toh kya dikhta hai?)
     3.  Har cheez ka sauda kya hai?
     4.  Apne jawab ko todne ki koshish karo.
     5.  Naksha banao, list nahi.
```
